payon,164,229,5	script	Elder Sanyul#illusion_moonlight	1_F_ORIENT_04,{
	if (BaseLevel < 100) {
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Mm, Eu tenho um sentindo algo suspicious is sobre to acontecer.";
		mes "I wish you were stronger. Entao, I would've been capaz de tell you sobre it.";
		next;
		mes "^4D4DFFVoce deve be Nivel 100 ou acimum to perform este missao.^000000";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
	OnStart:
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Este matter is out of nosso hands agora.";
		mes "We nao pode let it manter happening like this.";
		next;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Vamos esperar fou ^0000FFMuyeon^000000 agora.";
		mes "A segurancum is mais importante que anything else.";
		next;
		select("Com licenca.");
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Hm? Voce e um aventureiro.";
		mes "Voce acontecer to ouvir nosso conversation?";
		next;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "I ver.";
		mes "Talvez e faith esse you overheard us.";
		next;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Hum um cavernum perto o Arca Vilum in o back of Payon.";
		mes "E um haunted caverna, entao nos temos been preforming rituals e exorcism lum regularly.";
		next;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Mas o Nine-tailed foxes spirseu are mais enraged que we pensou.";
		mes "Cadum tempo alguem tries to preform um ritual to o fox beads, they pegar nightmares.";
		npctalk "It's as if the spirits were antagonized by them.","Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "E este tempo, she's completely lost a mente e entered o caverna.";
		mes "That's why nos estamos preocupado sobre her.";
		next;
		select("Is it sobre Muyeon?");
		npctalk "Maybe we should send someone else.","Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight",bc_self;
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Yes. Elum e o Soul Linker who performs o ritual regularly.";
		mes "Nos temos perguntou um soldado to encontrar her.";
		mes "They deve ser perto o cavernum by agora.";
		next;
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Voce poderium ir verificar up ^0000FFMuyeon^000000 fou us?";
		next;
		npctalk "Yes. could you?","",bc_self;
		if (select("Sure, I will:Desculpe, Eu sou afraid of ghost.") == 2) {
			mes "[ Sanyul ]";
			mes "Huh? We nao dizer anything sobre ghost. Ou did we?";
			close;
		}
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "I esperancum o soldado will encontrar a antes she goes deep dentro o ruined vila.";
		next;
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Pou favou ir to o cavernum e ver se Muyeon e o soldado are forum ja.";
		mes "A young scholar followed o soldado, mas she nao parecer reliable at todos.";
		next;
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Conversar to o scholar, talvez he could dar you um informacao on o situation.";
		illusion_moonlight = 1;
		setquest 7776;
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "I esperancum o soldado will encontrar a antes she goes deep dentro o ruined vila.";
		next;
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Pou favou ir to o cavernum e ver se Muyeon e o soldado are forum ja.";
		mes "A young scholar followed o soldado, mas she nao parecer reliable at todos.";
		next;
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "Conversar to o scholar, talvez he could dar you um informacao on o situation.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ Sanyul ]";
	mes "I esperancum tudo sera okay.";
	mes "Huh? Eu estavum conversando sobre o cavernum in o back.";
	mes "Eu sou preocupado sobre o que e happening la.";
	close;

OnInit:
	questinfo QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"BaseLevel >= 100 && illusion_moonlight < 1";
	end;
}

payon,167,229,3	script	Elder Jagyeom#illusion_moonlight	4_M_HUOLDARMY,{
	if (BaseLevel < 100) {
		mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
		mes "I esperancum tudo sera okay.";
		mes "Seeing esse voce e um aventureiro, I assume voce e familiar com o ^0000FFPayon Caverna^000000.";
		mes "Tenhum cuidado, algo ruim is happening dentro.";
		next;
		mes "^0000FFVoce deve be Nivel 100 ou acimum to perform este missao.";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
		doevent "Elder Sanyul#illusion_moonlight::OnStart";
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		mes "[ Sanyul ]";
		mes "Pou favou ir to o caverna's entrance in o Arca Vila.";
		mes "Ask o young scholar lum sobre Muyeon's whereabouts.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ Jagyeom ]";
	mes "Talvez e tempo we sair coisas to o younger generations.";
	mes "Hum nadum we velho folk could do.";
	close;
}

pay_arche,48,137,4	script	Young Scholar#ill_moon_arch	4_F_03,{
	if (illusion_moonlight == 0) {
		npctalk "Why aren't they coming out already...","",bc_self;
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 1) {
		npctalk "Why are they still not coming out...","",bc_self;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Ah, hoje deve ser meu lucky dia!";
		mes "Um aventureiro showed up, apenas when I needed one!";
		next;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Com licenca.";
		mes "Eu tenho ouviu aventureiros do muitos coisas fou others. Isentao e verdade?";
		next;
		select("It is, mas Eu sou pretty ocupado fou agora.:Decline.");
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "...Oh, I ver.";
		mes "Voce deve be ocupado.";
		mes "Eu sou desculpe I bothered you.";
		mes "By o caminho, what brings you aqui?";
		next;
		select("Voce jum visto alguem named Muyeon?");
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Muyeon? Voce significum Muyeon o Soul Linker?";
		mes "Bem, technically, she's aindum um apprentice, mas that's nao importante.";
		next;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Eu sou waiting fou a agorum mesmo.";
		mes "She rum parum dentro o caverna, as se she was possessed by algo.";
		next;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Junghee? I significa, um soldado followed a in, mas neita of them came out yet.";
		mes "Eu estavum debating se I should ir depois them.";
		next;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "I esperancum nadum ruim happens to esses two.";
		cloakoffnpcself( "Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Muyeon#ill_moon_arch" );
		sleep2 750;
		npctalk "There they are! They look fine!","Young Scholar#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 1500;
		npctalk "Ugh, I don't want to train anymore!","Muyeon#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		npctalk "...","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Chunghae, o que sao you fazendo out aqui?";
		npctalk "I was worried about you..","",bc_self;
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "You were preocupado sobre me, mas este is as longe you were willing to seguir me?";
		mes "Esquecer it. No matter how miserable I am, Eu nao precisar seu ajudar!";
		sleep2 500;
		npctalk "Now, that's enough.","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 500;
		npctalk "Th-this is unfair...","",bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Bye!","Muyeon#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Ah, Eu sou nao vai do este mais. Tell esse to o velho geezers!";
		next;
		npctalk "Sigh. I'll go after her. Talk to the Elders, will you?","Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch",bc_self;
		sleep2 1500;
		cloakonnpcself( "Payon Soldier#ill_moon_arch" );
		cloakonnpcself( "Muyeon#ill_moon_arch" );
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "That's....!";
		mes "She left sem dizendo us o que aconteceu.";
		next;
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Ah, Junghee!";
		mes "By o caminho aventureiro, nao eram you procurando fou Muyeon?";
		next;
		select("I guess Eu tenho to sair agora.");
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Muyeon must have gone to o ^0000CDDetached Payon Palace.^000000";
		mes "Eu sou indo lum do voce quer to vir com me?";
		next;
		illusion_moonlight = 2;
		erasequest 7776;
		setquest 7777;
        if (select("Ir com him.:Ir separately.") == 2) {
			mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
			mes "Muyeon must have gone to o ^0000CDDetached Payon Palace.^000000";
			mes "Why voce nao ir conversar to her?";
			mes "Eu vou conversar to o Elders.";
			close;
        }
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Entao vamos ir to o ^0000CDDetached Payon Palace.^000000";
        close2;
        warp "payon",105,320;
        end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 2) {
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Muyeon must have gone to o ^0000CDDetached Payon Palace.^000000";
		mes "Why voce nao ir conversar to her?";
		mes "Eu vou conversar to o Elders.";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight < 6) {
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Legend says um nine-tailed fox gained enlightment e se tornou um sacred creature, e esse Moonlight Flowers have descended de her.  Eu acho e um historium worth procurando parum dentro.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
	mes "Vamos ir investigar o ruined vila!";
	mes "Voce gostarium de like to vir com me?";
	next;
	if (select("Ir com him.:Ir separately.") == 2) {
		mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
		mes "Eu vou call Junghee, e we'll seguir you soon.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[ Young Scholar ]";
	mes "Vamos ir!";
	close2;
	warp "pay_dun03",145,43;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo QTYPE_QUEST,QMARK_YELLOW,"illusion_moonlight == 1";
	end;
}

payon_in03,33,95,3	script	Muyeon#ill_moon_pin	4_F_TAEKWON,{
	if (checkweight(25271,1) == 0 || (MaxWeight - Weight) < 1000) {
		mes "- Voce e carregando tambem muitos itens to proceed com este missao. -";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight < 2) {
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Se voce e procurando fou o teacher, she's in o outro room.";
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 2) {
		.@elder$ = "Jagyeom#ill_moon_pin";
		.@scholar$ = "Young Scholar#ill_moon_pin";
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,true;
		npctalk "Whatever you say, I'm not doing it anymore.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2500;
		npctalk "This is the third time I had nightmares. I can't take it anymore!","",bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		unittalk getcharid(3),"What nightmares?",bc_self;
		npctalk "What nightmares?",.@scholar$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "Nightmares about about the foxes? Nine tails and Moonlight flowers?","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "Those nightmares are just too horrifying. I don't want to experience it anymore.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "But we have no one else left to perform the ritual.",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "Everyone gave up after the first try. You did it three times.",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "Elder Jaegyom, nightmares weren't the only thing occurred to me this time.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "It was different from the other two times I performed the ritual.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "How different?",.@scholar$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "...","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "It felt as if the nightmares is happening in reality.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "What do you mean? You were only trying to give peace to the angered spirits of the nine tailed foxes.",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "What did you see in the cave?",.@elder$,bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "I can't explain what I saw. You have to see it to believe it.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 2000;
		npctalk "We need someone strong to go in there, like that adventurer over there, should go in the cave.","",bc_self;
		illusion_moonlight = 3;
		completequest 7777;
		setquest 7778;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_ALL,false;
		end;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 3) {
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Okay, Eu vou tell you o historia.";
		mes "O Payon Cavernum is usou to be um part of um vilum esse was buried debaixo um landslide.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "As voce pode guess muitos pessoas were buried com it, e o spirseu are cacando o caverna.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Alguns of them were o nine-tailed foxes. O foxes raivum is beyond normal.";
		mes "That's o razao we tem sido performing um ritual in regular basis.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Mas agorum o problemum is,";
		mes "O foxes spirseu are ficando mais resentful by tempo, e ninguem knows o razao why.";
		mes "Talvez o ritual is o razao they are furioso, e nao funcionando as we hoped it would.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "And, cadum tempo I performed o ritual, Eu tinhum nightmares sobre o resentful spirseu e wailing nine-tailed foxes.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Soul Linkers communicates com o spirseu fou um vivendo, Having nightmares is apenas occurs normally.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Entao when meu primeiro nightmare occurred, I pensou erum debaixo o mesmo boat as o others.";
		mes "Mas agorum Eu sei o difference, It tambem has nadum to do com meu health.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Whatever it is o razao esse agitates o spirseu of o nine-tailed foxes.";
		mes "It drove me de sanity e sent me vai o ruined vila.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "And, I saw algo.";
		mes "E um image of outro mundo, diferente de ours.";
		mes "We must shut esse lugar down, entao ninguem sera capaz de access it.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Eu tenho um uneasy sentindo esse um dreadful is vai pegar lugar.";
		mes "Ou talvez e jum levando lugar..";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "O vague image I saw is indistinguishable de hell.";
		mes "I saw it depois o ultimo ritual esse I did.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "I beg of you, nao ask me to retornar fazendo esse novamente.";
		mes "Eu sou nao mentally forte suficiente to handle such nightmares.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Much menos on o que e levando lugar dentro o caverna.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Se voce quer to ir, voce vai tem que ir sozinho e ver it fou yourself.";
		mes "I aindum carregar o fox bead esse I tentou to purify. Deixe-me saber when you are pronto.";
		illusion_moonlight = 4;
		completequest 7778;
		setquest 7779;
		close;
	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 4) {
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Voce e pronto?";
		next;
		if (select("Sim.:Nao yet.") == 2) {
			mes "[ Muyeon ]";
			mes "Deixe-me saber when voce e pronto.";
			close;
		}
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Okay, este fox bead is nao completely purged yet.";

		sleep2 1800;
		npctalk "Look at the other side of the Netherworld River.","",bc_self;
		sleep2 250;
		specialeffect EF_PORTAL5;
		specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			cloakoffnpcself( "Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i );

		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {

		}
		npctalk "Die!","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_0",bc_self;

		sleep2 100;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;
			sleep2 30;
		}
		sleep2 150;
		npctalk "Die!","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_1",bc_self;

			specialeffect EF_QUAKEBODY2,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;

		npctalk "Die!","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_2",bc_self;

		sleep2 150;
		npctalk "Die!","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_3",bc_self;
		sleep2 700;

		sleep2 150;

			sleep2 30;
		}
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++)
			npctalk "DIE!!!","Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i,bc_self;
		sleep2 1200;
		specialeffect EF_BLUELINE;
		sleep2 500;
		specialeffect EF_SCREEN_QUAKE;
		sleep2 1200;
		npctalk "Fly to the other side of the river.","",bc_self;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {
			specialeffect EF_MADNESS_RED,AREA,"Nine Tail Spirit#ill_moon_" + .@i;

		}

			sleep2 50;

		}
		sleep2 1000;
		for (.@i = 0; .@i < 4; .@i++) {

	}
	if (illusion_moonlight == 8) {

		mes "Eu sei eles sao ^0000FFdreamt by o Moonlight Flowers.^000000";

		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";

		mes "Bem, Eu vou let Chunghae figure it out.";
		mes "Alright, entao Eu gostarium de better ir pegar alguns rest.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "Agorum esse Eu sei esse esses nightmares realmente originated de Moonlight Flowers, I feel better.";
		mes "Eu consigo sleep at ultimo.";
		next;
		mes "[ Muyeon ]";
		mes "We nao vai precisar mais rituals umum vez we deal com o nightmares.";
		mes "Does este significum Eu sou gratis? Gratis at ultimo!";
		mes "Eu nao ate mesmo saber how to obrigado.";
		next;

		mes "Eu tenho encontrou este estranho stones while Eu estavum la. E nao muito,";

	mes "[ Muyeon ]";
	mes "Boum sorte investigating o phenomenon com Chunghae!";
	mes "Once o nightmares are gone, we nao vai precisar to perform o ritual mais!";
	next;
	mes "[ Muyeon ]";
	mes "Como pode I be sure, you ask?";

	end;

	disablenpc( "Dracula#ilgf02" );

		mapannounce "gef_d01_i", "Angry Dracula: Leave Bomi alone! Fight me instead!", bc_map, 0xFF0000;
	else
		mapannounce "gef_d01_i", "Angry Dracula: Bomi needs rest. I'll deal with you!", bc_map, 0xFF0000;
	killmonster "gef_d01_i", "ill_vampire_spawn_bomi::OnMobDead";
	killmonster "gef_d01_i", "ill_vampire_spawn_dracula::OnMobDead";
	monster "gef_d01_i",0,0,"Angry Dracula",3757,1,"ill_vampire_spawn_dracula::OnMobDead";
	end;

// boss summoning - 1 day of delay for now
ice_d03_i,126,126,3	script	Orb of Seal#frozen1	CLEAR_NPC,{
	if (.ill_frozen_boss_timer >= gettimetick(2)) {
		mes "Este orb needs mais tempo to reunir suficiente energy antes it'll trabalhar.";
		close;
	}
	.@i = atoi( charat(strnpcinfo(2), 6) );
	.timer[.@i] = 30;
	initnpctimer;	// the effect continue after boss spawn
	if (.timer[1] > 0 && .timer[2] > 0 && .timer[3] > 0 && .timer[4] > 0) {
		.ill_frozen_boss_timer = gettimetick(2) + 86400;// respawn timer (seems to be one day)
		donpcevent "bosseff#frozen::OnEvent";
	}
	end;
OnTimer1000:
	.@i = atoi( charat(strnpcinfo(2), 6) );
	.timer[.@i]--;
	if (.timer[.@i] < 1)
		stopnpctimer;
	else {
		initnpctimer;
		specialeffect EF_DETOXICATION;
	}
	end;
}
ice_d03_i,172,126,3	duplicate(Orb of Seal#frozen1)	Orb of Seal#frozen2	CLEAR_NPC

ice_d03_i,149,112,0	script	bosseff#frozen	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,{
	end;
OnEvent:
	initnpctimer;
	end;
OnTimer1000:
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "All the energy that was sealed inside the cave has been released, awakening Ktullanux.", bc_map, 0xFFFF00;
	end;
OnTimer3000:
	stopnpctimer;
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "Ktullanux: Who dares call me a lizard? I'll freeze you all!", bc_map, 0xFFFF00;
	specialeffect EF_PORTAL;
	if (mobcount("ice_d03_i", "bosseff#frozen::OnBossDead") < 1)
		monster "ice_d03_i",149,112,"Awakened Ktullanux",3796,1, "bosseff#frozen::OnBossDead";	// ILL_KTULLANUX
	end;
OnBossDead:
	mapannounce "ice_d03_i", "Ktullanux: I'll... be... back...", bc_map;
	end;
}

	mes "Se you encontrar Leiphen, pou favou fazer sure he is comendo.";
	close2;
	emotion ET_KEK;
	warp "tur_d03_i",132,187;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 0 && BaseLevel > 149" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 11" );
	end;
}

OnTalk:

		npctalk "I will get payback... on... " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_ASSULTER", MOB_NAME) + "... They took my treasure!";
		break;
	}
	.talk = 0;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle == 3" );

	// daily
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16105,PLAYTIME) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_turtle > 10 && checkquest(16109,HUNTING) == 2 && countitem(25316) > 4" );
	end;
}

com_d02_i,1,1,1	script	com_d02_i_init_shadow_npc	-1,{
	end;
OnInit:
OnHour00:	// updated every 3h at a fixed time
OnHour03:
OnHour06:
OnHour09:
OnHour12:
OnHour15:
OnHour18:
OnHour21:
	setarray .@num[1],1,2,3,4,5,6,7;
	.@today = gettime(DT_DAYOFWEEK);
	.@today = (.@today == 0 ? 7 : .@today);	// SUNDAY - 0
	deletearray .@num[.@today],1;
	deletearray .@num[ rand(1,6) ],1;	// note: should the previous enabled vortex be taken into account?

	for ( .@i = 1; .@i < 6; ++.@i )
		disablenpc "Shimmer#Lu0" + .@num[.@i];
	end;
}

com_d02_i,266,76,6	duplicate(dummy_cloaked_npc)	Silhouette#LuE092	4_GHOST_STAND


// Daily quests
com_d02_i,238,266,4	script	Request Board#Lu	4_BOARD3,{
	if (checkweight(1201,3) == 0) {
		mes "- You nao pode proceed porque you have tambem muitos itens. -";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_luanda == 0) {
		mes "E um quadro de avisos com coisas requested by o vilum written on it, mas it nao parecer to be o case agora.";
		close;
	}

	.@quest_menu$[0] = callsub( S_MenuGather, 5848,5849, "Evidence of eradication of Megaliths" );
	.@quest_menu$[1] = callsub( S_MenuGather, 5850,5851, "Evidence of eradication of Wootan" );
	.@quest_menu$[2] = callsub( S_MenuHunt, 5852,5853, "Operation to eradicate Megaliths" );
	.@quest_menu$[3] = callsub( S_MenuHunt, 5854,5855, "Operation to eradicate Wootan" );

	.@playtime_5849 = checkquest(5849,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5851 = checkquest(5851,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5853 = checkquest(5853,PLAYTIME);
	.@playtime_5855 = checkquest(5855,PLAYTIME);

	// menu to take all quests at once
	if (.@playtime_5849 > 0 || .@playtime_5851 > 0 || .@playtime_5853 > 0 || .@playtime_5855 > 0) {	// at least one of the quests is in cooldown
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "^777777[Waiting] Receive all quests^000000";
		.@all_quests = 0;
	}
	else if (isbegin_quest(5848) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5850) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5852) > 0 || isbegin_quest(5854) > 0) {	// at least one of the quests is in progress
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "^777777[Progress] Receive all quests^000000";
		.@all_quests = 1;
	}
	else {	// can take all quests
		.@quest_menu$[4] = "[Daily] Accept all quests";
		.@all_quests = 2;
	}

	switch( select( .@quest_menu$[0], .@quest_menu$[1], .@quest_menu$[2], .@quest_menu$[3], .@quest_menu$[4] ) ) {
	case 1:
		switch( .@playtime_5849 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "Este missao cum be done novamente amanha.";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( isbegin_quest(5848) ) {
		case 0:
			mes "O vilum is having um lot of trouble com o Megaliths. Eu vou recompensum you se you trazer me 3 " + getitemname(25638) + " as proof of defeating o Megaliths.";
			mes "  ";
			mes "Chief";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do nao aceitar" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5848;
			open_quest_ui(5848);
			mes "Seu request tem sido recebeu. Pou favou relatorio to ^FF0000Defense Manager Luanda^000000.";
			close;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			mes "^FF0000Evidence of eradication of Megaliths^000000";
			mes "Seu request is in progress.";
			mes "Voce pode cancel. Porem, se you cancel, o progress made entao longe sera lost. Voce e sure voce quer to cancel?";
			next;
			if (select( "Do nao cancel", "Cancelar" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5848;
			mes "O request tem sido cancelled.";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 2:
		switch( .@playtime_5851 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "Este missao cum be done novamente amanha.";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( isbegin_quest(5850) ) {
		case 0:
			mes "Wootum tribes are causing um lot of trouble in o vila. Eu vou recompensum you se you trazer 3 " + getitemname(25633) + " as proof esse you have defeated them.";
			mes "  ";
			mes "Chief";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do nao aceitar" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5850;
			open_quest_ui(5850);
			mes "Seu request tem sido recebeu. Pou favou relatorio to ^FF0000Defense Manager Luanda^000000.";
			close;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			mes "^FF0000Evidence of extermination of Wootan^000000";
			mes "Seu request is in progress.";
			mes "Voce pode cancel. Porem, se you cancel, o progress made entao longe sera lost. Voce e sure voce quer to cancel?";
			next;
			if (select( "Do nao cancel", "Cancelar" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5850;
			mes "O request tem sido cancelled.";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		switch( .@playtime_5853 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "Este missao cum be done novamente amanha.";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(5852,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "Nos temos planned to subdue o megaliths. Anyone who cum catch 5 " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TRI_JOINT",MOB_NAME) + ", " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STALACTIC_GOLEM",MOB_NAME) + ", e " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_MEGALITH",MOB_NAME) + ", pou favou apply.";
			mes "  ";
			mes "Chief";
			next;
			if (select( "Accept request", "Do nao aceitar" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5852;
			open_quest_ui(5852);
			mes "Seu request tem sido recebeu. Pou favou relatorio to ^FF0000Defense Manager Luanda^000000.";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "^FF0000 Megalith extermination^000000";
			mes "Seu request is in progress.";
			mes "Voce pode cancel. Porem, se you cancel, o progress made entao longe sera lost. Voce e sure voce quer to cancel?";
			next;
			if (select( "Do nao cancel", "Cancelar" ) == 1)
				end;

			close;
		}
		end;
	case 4:
		switch( .@playtime_5855 ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "Este missao cum be done novamente amanha.";
			close;
		case 2:
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(5854,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "Nos temos planned to subdue o Wutum tribe. Anyone who cum catch 5 " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_STONE_SHOOTER",MOB_NAME) + ", " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_WOOTAN_SHOOTER",MOB_NAME) + ", e " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_WOOTAN_FIGHTER",MOB_NAME) + ", pou favou apply.";
			mes "  ";
			mes "Chief";
			next;

				end;
			setquest 5854;
			open_quest_ui(5854);
			mes "Seu request tem sido recebeu. Pou favou relatorio to ^FF0000Defense Manager Luanda^000000.";
			close;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "^FF0000Evidence of eradication of megaliths^000000";
			mes "Seu request is in progress.";
			mes "Voce pode cancel. Porem, se you cancel, o progress made entao longe sera lost. Voce e sure voce quer to cancel?";
			next;
			if (select( "Do nao cancel", "Cancelar" ) == 1)
				end;
			erasequest 5854;
			mes "O request tem sido cancelled.";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "Voce pode receber o recompensum fou este missao de Defense Manager Luanda.";
			close;
		}
		end;
	case 5:
		switch( .@all_quests ) {
		case 0:	// at least one of the quests is in cooldown
			mes "Hum um missao whose cooldown has nao passed.";
			close;
		case 1:	// at least one of the quests is in progress
			mes "Alguns missoes are in progress.";
			close;
		case 2:	// can take all quests
			mes "Voce e sure voce quer to receber todos missoes on o Missao Board?";
			next;
			if (select( "Aceitar", "Do nao aceitar" ) == 2)
				end;
			setquest 5848;
			setquest 5850;
			setquest 5852;
			setquest 5854;
			mes "Todos missoes on o missao quadro tem sido recebeu.";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

S_MenuGather:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( isbegin_quest(getarg(0)) ) {
		case 0:
			.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
			break;
		case 1:
		case 2:
			// note: the amount of item is not checked
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Progress] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[Waiting]" + .@string$ + "^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		erasequest getarg(1);
		.@menu$ = "[Daily]" + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;

S_MenuHunt:
	.@string$ = getarg(2);

	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(0),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Progress] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@menu$ = "^777777[Reward] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@menu$ = "^777777[Waiting] " + .@string$ + "^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		erasequest getarg(1);
		.@menu$ = "[Daily] " + .@string$;
		break;
	}
	return .@menu$;
}

		next;
		mes "[Kena]";
		mes "It seria perigoentao se left unattended...";
		close;
	}
	end;

S_Quest:
	switch( checkquest(getarg(1),PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		switch( checkquest(getarg(2),HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			.@string$ = getarg(0);
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			.@string$ = getarg(0) + " - In Progress";
			break;
		case 2:
			.@string$ = getarg(0) + " - Done";
			break;
		}
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		.@string$ = "^999999" + getarg(0) + " - Pending^000000";
		break;
	case 2:
		.@string$ = getarg(0);
		break;
	}
	return .@string$;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3418,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(3417,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3417,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3418,PLAYTIME) == 2" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3420,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(3419,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3419,HUNTING) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(3420,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	end;
}

			close;
		case 2:
			erasequest 8738;
			break;
		}
		if (isbegin_quest(8737) == 0) {
			mes "[Mirko]";
			mes "I picked up um unknown shell o outro dia. It parece esse todos o monstros in o Garden of Perception, like ants, have them.";
			mes "Voce pode coletar sobre 20 ^0000FF" + getitemname(1000506) + "^000000?";
			next;
			if (select( "Aceitar", "Sair" ) == 2) {
				mes "[Mirko]";
				mes "I nao pode";
				mes "Hoje, Eu tenho no choice mas to commission outro aventureiros.";
				close;
			}
			mes "[Mirko]";
			mes "Okay, esperar.";
			setquest 8737;
			close;
		}
		if (countitem(1000506) < 20) {
			mes "[Mirko]";
			mes "Cacar o monstros de o Garden of Perception";
			mes "e coletar 20 ^0000FF" + getitemname(1000506) + "^000000.";
			close;
		}
		delitem 1000506,20;	// Shell_Of_Cognition
		erasequest 8737;
		setquest 8738;
		mes "[Mirko]";
		mes "Bom job!";
		mes "Entao Eu vou se cuide of you amanhum tambem.";
		getitem 25271,1;	// IllusionStone
		getexp 3113916,2347020;
		getexp 934174,704106;
		close;
	case 6:
		end;
	}
	end;

		mes "- Lighten seu weight e -";
		mes "- tentar novamente. -";
		close;
	}
	if (ill_teddy < 9) {
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Ah...";
		mes "Isentao e um sonho ou reality...";
		mes "Am I morto... ou am I vivo...";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "We're... nao supposed to volte...";
		mes "Eu tenho to ir back...";
		close;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Whoops...nao rush me like that...";
		close;
	case 2:
		erasequest 11569;
		break;
	}
	switch( checkquest(11567,HUNTING) ) {
	case -1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Ah...";
		mes "Isentao e um sonho ou reality...";
		mes "Am I morto... ou am I vivo...";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "We're... nao supposed to volte...";
		mes "Eu tenho to ir back...";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Hey... nao apenas ste la, me ajude...";
		mes "Me... o Nazarians...";
		next;
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "^ff0000Kill 10 " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_R", MOB_NAME) + ", " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_Y", MOB_NAME) + ", " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_G", MOB_NAME) + ", " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_B", MOB_NAME) + ", e " + getmonsterinfo("ILL_TEDDY_BEAR_W", MOB_NAME) + "^000000 e retornar them to o depths... to rest...";
		next;
		if (select( "Aceitar", "Sair" ) == 2) {
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "...Ah...";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Eu quero to end this... infernal nightmare...";
		mes "Vir on...";
		setquest 11567;
		setquest 11568;
		close;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
		mes "Ah... When will o peaceful rest...";
		mes "...is o request yet...";
		close;
	case 2:
		switch( checkquest(11568,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			end;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "Ah... When will o peaceful rest...";
			mes "...is o request yet...";
			close;
		case 2:
			mes "[Gloomy Teddy Bear]";
			mes "Pou que it ainda... nao indo embora...";
			mes "Este horrible nightmare...";
			erasequest 11567;
			erasequest 11568;
			setquest 11569;
			getitem 25271,1; // IllusionStone
			getexp 1300000,1000000;
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ill_teddy == 9 && checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) == -1 && checkquest(11567,HUNTING) == -1 && checkquest(11568,HUNTING) == -1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11569,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(11567,HUNTING) == 2 && checkquest(11568,HUNTING) == 2" );
	end;
}
